      - **(e)/(f) on a mid-yield-orphaned initial dispatch — server-proxy-only (round-24 SD-23 spec-coherence; resolves SD-23-B1↔ai#6, CD-ruled IMPORTANT).** The dispatch-origin discriminator (the line-222 rule) requires a mid-yield-orphaned **initial** dispatch (player departed between the step-3 yield and the step-5 commit) to run the full initial set including (e)/(f). This is **not a destroyed-instance crash**: (e) emits only the **server-side camera-state signal** (the spectator-camera-activate event observed on the server) and (f) writes only the **server-side input-lock flag** — both are server proxy-state. Any client-directed RemoteEvent these would normally also send is a **silent no-op to a departed player** on Roblox, and **neither path dereferences `player.Character`** (the orphaned-commit rule at the Orphaned-row exception below already forbids touching the live `Player` instance). So the initial set on an orphan runs its (e)/(f) proxies without error — distinct from a reconciliation orphan, which suppresses (c)–(f) entirely. H.75 asserts (e)/(f) via these server-observable proxies.
      - (g) **`PredatorService:ReleasePredatorLock(player)`** (SD-B2 / AI-1 — drop any predator target-lock / aggro held on the dying player; the F.4 B-AI-2 contract). **Mandatory in BOTH paths** — a dead (S4) or removed (Path B) player is not a valid lock target; leaving the lock held is the wasted-aggro coherence bug F.4 names, and is incoherent with T6 clearing `lastPredatorDamageTimestamp` + `predatorCausedImminent`. Like (a)/(b) it is idempotent (releasing an already-released lock is a no-op) and is **re-invoked on a reconciliation-success path** alongside (a)/(b), so a death whose initial charge rolled back still releases the lock once the tick commits. This row mirrors the `:ReleasePredatorLock` entry already in the C.6 T5 transition table — the two are now consistent (the round-11 SD-B2/AI-1 finding was that this enum omitted it). **Round-18 [R16-2]: on the INITIAL T5 dispatch (g) fires PRE-YIELD at the step-2 transition (the pre-yield ordering rule above), NOT here in the post-`Committed` block — it is listed in this enumeration for completeness of the side-effect set and because it is also the side-effect a reconciliation tick re-fires idempotently;**
      - (h) **`PredatorService:OnPlayerS4Entered(player)` ([R16-2], user-ruled 2026-06-11 — Path A only)**: the server-internal cross-service lifecycle signal that tells PredatorService the player has entered S4, so the F.4 S4 no-re-acquire gate has a mechanism (the frozen corpse `HumanoidRootPart` lingers 30 s and is position-indistinguishable from a live player — position alone cannot drive the gate). Fired at the T5 state transition itself (the S4-entry fact), NOT gated on the oxygen commit — the predator must stop treating the corpse as a target immediately, independent of the charge's success. **Round-18 [R16-2]: "at the transition" is normatively PRE-YIELD — fired in the step-2 synchronous span before the step-3 `RequestSquadOxygenSpend` yield (the pre-yield ordering rule above), so the S4 no-re-acquire gate is armed before any yield and never depends on cross-service `PlayerRemoving` teardown ordering.** **Path A only**: a Path-B player is removed, never in S4; PredatorService's own `PlayerRemoving` teardown covers the removed case. Idempotent; NOT re-invoked on a reconciliation-tick dispatch (the S4 entry already happened — H.70);
      - (i) **`OnSquadMemberAliveChanged(player.UserId, isAlive=false)` (round-16 Crafting consumer-obligation adoption — both paths)**: the alive-state edge Crafting's `RunSession.aliveMembers` contract names PC the canonical publisher of (Crafting C.10/F.4). Fired at the T5 state transition itself (the death/disconnect is a fact of the run, not of the charge) — **round-18 [R16-2]: normatively PRE-YIELD, in the step-2 synchronous span before the step-3 yield (pre-yield ordering rule above), so Crafting's alive-set sees the `isAlive=false` edge without waiting on the oxygen charge**. Crafting's own `Humanoid.Died`/`Players.PlayerRemoving` connections also drain its alive-set; the dual source is harmless — Crafting's buffered departure drain is idempotent and de-duped per tick. NOT re-invoked on a reconciliation-tick dispatch (H.70). The `isAlive=true` edge fires at T6 — see the C.6 T6 row.

      **Path B's invocation of T5 is identical to Path A's except where the player's removal makes a side-effect inapplicable (rows e and f). The player-removal-safe subset — pulse-timer resets (a, b) AND the predator-lock release (g) — is mandatory in both paths.** Items (a) and (b) close phantom-emission classes against `HumanoidRootPart` instances that no longer exist (Pillar 1); item (g) closes the wasted-aggro / stale-lock class.

   **Set-before-yield is the whole guarantee.** Step 2 (`Pending → InFlight`) completes before any yield, so whichever death path fires second resolves the record at step 1, reads `InFlight`/`Committed`, and returns — even if the first path is mid-yield in steps 3–4. Without this (e.g. invoking `RequestSquadOxygenSpend` before the transition), both paths could observe `Pending` across the yield boundary and double-deduct. **Clear-on-failure (step 4 failure → `Pending`) is the inverse**: the `InFlight` transition is a *commitment* that the deduction will be attempted; the rollback releases it when the attempt provably failed, handing the row to the tick. Together they form a try-or-rollback transaction at the rule level.

   Idempotency holds against: (a) `Humanoid.Died` and `PlayerRemoving` firing in the same or adjacent Heartbeat (the InFlight guard closes this), (b) `PlayerRemoving` for a player already in S4 (the record is already `InFlight`/`Committed`), (c) network-desync replay of either event, (d) a reconciliation tick racing a late second death-path firing (same InFlight guard). **Exactly one squad oxygen unit is deducted per player-death-event regardless of path.**

   **Cross-system dependency**: `lastPredatorDamageTimestamp` AND the `predatorCausedImminent` latch (B5 arm 3) are **server-internal tables written by `PredatorService` on damage application**. Player Controller reads both; PredatorService owns both. Forward obligations (including the latch's full set/clear lifecycle and the `:ReleasePredatorLock` contract, B-AI-2) are flagged in F.4.

   **Implementation requirement**: T5 MUST be a callable procedure independent of the `Humanoid.Died` signal path so Path B can invoke it from `PlayerRemoving`. Flagged as a new ADR ("Death-Cost Attribution") in F.4.
4. **Inputs accepted while dead** (`Dead-Respawning` state):
   - **Quick-ping**: **NO at MVP — CUT pending the Camera GDD (R17-3, user-ruled 2026-06-14)**. A dead player's ping (especially a Danger ping) has no spectator visual treatment authored (V/A.2 has no greyed/ghost/skull dead-ping marker) and the Camera/spectator GDD that would govern the dead-player experience is unauthored (OQ.3) — so a live squad would read a *dead* teammate's ping as live survival information (round-19 ux-B2 / game-I6). Until the Camera GDD authors the dead-spectator ping treatment, `RequestPing` is **rejected from S4/S5** (C.9 validation). Re-enabling dead-player pings is a **Camera-GDD forward obligation (F.4)**, not a PC MVP feature.
   - **Emote wheel**: YES (dead players still signal the squad — an emote is a non-locating squad-social signal, not a survival-information marker, so it does not carry the dead-ping misread risk). **World-space billboard SUPPRESSED while dead (round-24 UX-B2, user-ruled 2026-06-15):** an emote fired from S4 renders ONLY as a **non-positional squad-social UI signal** (the squad roster / spectator-overlay entry for that player) — it MUST NOT render the live-player world-space emote billboard over the dead player's corpse. A billboard pinned to the corpse `HumanoidRootPart` (which lingers the full 30 s `RESPAWN_DELAY`, V/A.1) would be a **dead-player location tell — exactly the survival-information misread that got S4 `RequestPing` CUT (R17-3)** — so rendering the emote billboard while suppressing the ping would reopen that hole. Suppressing the world-space billboard is what keeps the "non-locating" claim honest. Re-enabling any world-space dead-emote treatment is gated on the same Camera-GDD dead-spectator distinguishability work as the ping re-enable (F.4 Camera row, OQ.3). Verified by H.80.
   - **Spectator camera**: YES (free-roam within squad proximity — owned by a future Camera GDD; Player Controller surfaces the input affordance only)
   - **Movement / sprint**: NO (no body)
   - **Lantern toggle**: NO (no body)
   - **Gather / interact / craft**: NO (no body)
5. **Sprint state hard-reset on death**: when the death flow triggers, the server MUST clear sprint state (force-walk, stop sprint timer, stop Sprint emission interval). On respawn, the player spawns in `Alive-Walking` with stamina restored to `STAMINA_MAX` and lantern in `LanternLowered`. *Pitfall mitigation: prevents the player from respawning already-sprinting and broadcasting disturbance before they've oriented.*
6. **Spectator camera and StreamingEnabled**: dead-player spectate camera following a teammate into an unloaded chunk MUST guard against missing geometry. Recommended fallback: freeze the camera until `workspace:HasChunkLoaded()` confirms (verify API name against current Roblox docs before architecture). *Alternative behavior is OK; the requirement is "no spectator falls into the void" — see Section E for edge case.*
7. **Respawn anchor**: a level-design-flagged spawn point (NOT random, NOT the death point). Specific anchors are level-design's responsibility.
8. **Whole-squad-dead → run end (and `RunEnded` authority — round-9 B4, CD-ruled)**: if all 2–4 squad members enter `Dead-Respawning` simultaneously *and* the squad oxygen pool is too low to fund any respawn, all players transition to `Dead-Permanent` (per game-concept "If the entire squad dies, the run ends") and the round-results screen takes over. **`RunEnded` has exactly ONE authoritative broadcaster.** Player Controller (T7 wipe) and Crafting & Items (T8 Beacon victory / window-fail) can each *detect* a run-end condition — at the 2-player floor a full-squad wipe during the Beacon window is a common coincidence where both would otherwise fire. **PC raises its T7 whole-squad-wipe condition to the single arbiter via `RunController:RunEndConditionRaised("wipe", squadState)` (caller-side contract in F.4); PC does NOT itself broadcast `RunEnded`.** **[R16-1] (user-ruled 2026-06-11): NEITHER PC NOR CRAFTING broadcasts `RunEnded` — the RunController arbiter is the SOLE broadcaster, and ALL run-end conditions are *raised* to it: PC's T7 wipe, PC's T8 victory (consuming Crafting's `OnBeaconWindowSurvived(timestamp)`), and Crafting's BCT-DEFEAT in-window defeats, which Crafting raises to the arbiter DIRECTLY (not relayed through PC; the matching one-line prose-pointer landed in Crafting's C.10 `runOutcomeResolved` schema this round).** **BC4 all-dead suppression (round-16 Crafting adoption — supersedes "defeat-hold covers it")**: PC's T7 wipe raise is SUPPRESSED for the **whole post-activation lifecycle** — while `beaconActivated == true` (BCT3 → `RunEnded`; PC tracks this via a server-internal `_beaconActivated` latch set on its `OnEscapeBeaconActivated(activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` subscription and cleared on the arbiter's `RunEnded` broadcast — which PC receives via the inbound subscription declared after C.9 (round-20 keystone) — **or, as a Pillar-4 backstop if that broadcast never arrives, by the `BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT` self-heal (C.6 latch table, R17-4)**; mirroring Crafting's `RunSession.beaconActivated` lifecycle) — because once the Beacon is active, **Crafting's BC4 drain owns the wipe terminal** (its BCT-DEFEAT raises to the arbiter); PC's all-dead raise stays authoritative ONLY pre-activation. The 0.5 s `RUN_END_DEFEAT_HOLD` covers adjacent-tick skew only and is NOT the suppression mechanism (the prior text leaned on it alone — H.61). The arbiter resolves the broadcast once, with a fixed precedence: **a resolved T8 victory (squad survived the Beacon window → Crafting's `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`, BCT4) outranks a coincident T7 wipe** (the squad survived — a simultaneous wipe does not retroactively turn a survived window into a defeat; a player already in S4 at window-survived is rescued per T8). **Beacon activation alone (`OnEscapeBeaconActivated`, BCT3) is NOT a victory and never resolves a `RunEnded`** — it only starts the window (R11-D1 / GD-02). Until a `RunController` (or equivalent) is authored, the single-broadcaster requirement + the victory-over-wipe precedence is a **forward obligation flagged for architecture in F.4** (PC's `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` consumer and PC's wipe-condition emitter must route through one owner). This closes round-8 game-designer B4 (PC↔Crafting double-broadcast of `RunEnded`).

### States and Transitions

#### C.6 — State Machine

Player Controller runs **two orthogonal state machines** that compose freely. The locomotion-axis (walk/sprint) and the lantern-axis (lowered/raised) are independent; both may be active simultaneously, each with its own emission interval timer and movement-delta tracker. The death-axis is a top-level state that pre-empts both.

**States:**

| State ID | Axis | Name | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | Locomotion | Alive-Walking | Default. WalkSpeed = `WALK_SPEED`. No Sprint emission. |
| S2 | Locomotion | Alive-Sprinting | WalkSpeed = `SPRINT_SPEED`. Stamina draining. Sprint emission on interval. |
| S3a | Lantern | LanternLowered | Default. No Light emission. |
| S3b | Lantern | LanternRaised | Light emission on interval. Composable with S1 or S2. |
| S4 | Death | Dead-Respawning | 30 s countdown. Movement / lantern / gather denied. **Emote + spectate allowed; ping CUT at MVP (R17-3 — pending Camera GDD, C.5.3 item 4 / C.9 `RequestPing`).** **Dead emote renders squad-UI-only — world-space billboard over the corpse SUPPRESSED via the server-minted `OnEmoteBroadcast.renderScope == "squad-ui"` discriminator (round-28 T-1/T-2 NET-2 closure; round-24 UX-B2, C.5.4 item 2 / V/A.2 / H.80).** |
| S5 | Death | Dead-Permanent | Whole-squad-dead terminal. All inputs locked. Run-results screen. |

**Jump** is treated as a transient physics event, not a discrete state — it does not affect emission timing or the locomotion axis.

**Transitions:**

| ID | Trigger | From | To | Side Effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1 | Sprint input asserted + stamina > 0 + alive | S1 | S2 | Server: WalkSpeed → SPRINT_SPEED; first SprintEmission at full magnitude (within `FIRST_PULSE_GRACE_WINDOW`); start sprint pulse interval timer |
| T2 | Sprint input released OR stamina hits 0 | S2 | S1 | Server: WalkSpeed → WALK_SPEED; stop sprint pulse interval timer; start `STAMINA_REGEN_DELAY` timer |
| T3 | Lantern toggle input + alive | S3a | S3b | Server: first LightEmission at full magnitude (within `FIRST_PULSE_GRACE_WINDOW`); start light pulse interval timer |
| T4 | Lantern toggle input | S3b | S3a | Server: stop light pulse interval timer; no emission on exit |
| T5 | `Humanoid.Died` fires (idempotent) | S1 or S2 | S4 | Server: stamina frozen at current value; force T4 if S3b active; force T2 if S2 active; **deduct 1 squad oxygen unit via the C.5.3 `DeathCostReconciliation` dispatch** (mint the death-event record, `Pending → InFlight → Committed`; rollback-and-tick on failure); **release predator target-lock / aggro on this player** (F.4 B-AI-2 — `PredatorService:ReleasePredatorLock(player)`); reset `t_lastGracePulse_sprint = nil` and `t_lastGracePulse_light = nil` (Pillar 4 fairness — fresh grace slate post-respawn); spectator camera activated; client receives `OnPlayerDied`; client's local input handler locks movement+lantern+sprint+gather; **fire `PredatorService:OnPlayerS4Entered(player)` ([R16-2] — the S4 no-re-acquire mechanism; C.5.3 side-effect (h), Path A only)**; **fire `OnSquadMemberAliveChanged(playerId, isAlive=false)` (round-16 Crafting adoption; C.5.3 side-effect (i), both paths)** |
| T6 | Respawn timer = 30 s elapsed AND squad oxygen pool ≥ 1 *(if the pool is < 1 at timer-elapse and no wipe, the transition is WITHHELD — the player remains in S4 and this condition is re-checked each server Heartbeat; see the E.E oxygen-gated respawn wait, H.65)* | S4 | S1 (with S3a) | Server: respawn at level-flagged anchor; stamina = `STAMINA_MAX`; lantern = `LanternLowered`; sprint state cleared; emit no disturbance; camera returns to player; **clear `currentDeathRecord[player] = nil`** (detach the player from the prior life's record so the next life starts fresh) AND **remove that prior-life record from `reconcileRows` IF it is `Committed`** (its charge is final and the respawn closes the replay window, N-2) — **an uncommitted `Pending`/`InFlight` prior-life record is NOT deleted; it survives by `deathEventId` and reconciles post-respawn** (C.5.3); **reset `lastServerTrackedPosition[player]` to the respawn-anchor position** (AI-3 — so the position cache is fresh for the new life from the instant of respawn, never the 30 s-stale death-point value; it is NOT cleared to `nil`, which would lose the nil-coalesce fallback); **fire `PredatorService:OnPlayerT6Respawned(player)` ([R16-2])** — this signal is the **SINGLE trigger channel (round-16 AI-I1)** through which PredatorService executes the T6 clearance of `lastPredatorDamageTimestamp[player]`, `predatorCausedImminent[player]`, AND `predatorCausedImminentSetTimestamp[player]` (paired reset) plus the **predator state-flush** (drop any remembered prior position / search focus for this player — the respawned player is a new body at a new anchor, never re-acquired from death-point memory); this C.6 row is the NORMATIVE trigger-site enumeration — the F.4 lifecycle bullets document the obligation and defer here; **fire `OnSquadMemberAliveChanged(playerId, isAlive=true)` (round-16 Crafting adoption) — AFTER the respawn placement completes (the respawned `HumanoidRootPart` exists AND is positioned at the anchor), preserving Crafting's positioned-HRP re-add guarantee (Crafting C.10 `_pendingReadds`)** |
| T7 | All squad in S4 AND squad oxygen < 1 **AND NOT `_beaconActivated`** (round-16 BC4 all-dead suppression — post-activation the wipe terminal is Crafting's BCT-DEFEAT; C.5.8, H.61) | S4 (all players) | S5 (all players) | Server: **PC raises the whole-squad-wipe condition via `RunController:RunEndConditionRaised("wipe", squadState)` (F.4 — PC does NOT itself broadcast `RunEnded`; [R16-1]: neither does Crafting — the arbiter is the sole broadcaster)**; **while `_beaconActivated == true` this raise is SUPPRESSED for the whole post-activation lifecycle — the in-window wipe resolves via Crafting's BCT-DEFEAT raise and reaches PC as the arbiter's `RunEnded(defeat)` broadcast, which drives the same S4→S5 transition + input lock (H.61)**; the arbiter holds it for the `RUN_END_DEFEAT_HOLD` window before broadcasting defeat. On the arbiter's broadcast: round-results screen; lock all inputs. If a T8 `"victory"` condition resolves during the hold window, **victory takes precedence** and the wipe is discarded (C.5.8 + F.4 precedence + defeat-hold rule) |
| T8 | Squad survives the Beacon window (Beacon-driven, owned by Crafting & Items GDD) | any alive OR S4 | S5 (victory) | Server: the single `RunEnded` arbiter broadcasts `RunEnded(victory)`; **PC consumes `OnBeaconWindowSurvived(timestamp)` (Crafting BCT4 — the survival-window-end victory emit; arity per Crafting C.14/C.15, adopted round-16), NOT `OnEscapeBeaconActivated`** (R11-D1 / GD-02 — see C.5.8). **Beacon *activation* (`OnEscapeBeaconActivated`, BCT3) is the window-START cue, explicitly NOT the victory trigger** per `crafting-and-items.md` C.9; victory fires only once the squad survives the window to BCT4. PC does not broadcast. Lock all inputs. **A player in S4 (Dead-Respawning) at window-survived is rescued by the squad: S4 → S5(victory), respawn timer cancelled, counted as a survivor of the run.** Victory takes precedence over a coincident T7 wipe |
| T9 | Emote wheel opened (touch only) | any | (locks tap-hold gather) | Client: gather input on right-thumb zone is suppressed until wheel closes. Not a state transition — a temporary input lock orthogonal to the state axes. |

**Concurrency invariants:**

- S2 (Alive-Sprinting) is mutually exclusive with S1 (Alive-Walking).
- S3a (LanternLowered) is mutually exclusive with S3b (LanternRaised).
- S2 + S3b is the maximum-loud state (both pulse streams active concurrently).
- S4 (Dead-Respawning) and S5 (Dead-Permanent) pre-empt the locomotion axis: while in S4 or S5, the locomotion state is implicitly S1 (the body is absent; the value is held but unused).

**Server-internal run-lifecycle latch — `_beaconActivated` (round-18 [R16-1] fanout — was prose-only in C.5.8/F.2):** in addition to the per-player axes above, `PlayerControllerService` tracks ONE server-internal run-lifecycle latch that conditions the T7 wipe-raise. It is **not** a player state and pre-empts nothing — it gates whether T7's raise side-effect fires. A developer building the T7/T8 rows must see its full set/clear lifecycle here, not just the reads in T7:

| Latch | Init | SET → `true` | CLEARED → `false` |
|---|---|---|---|
| `_beaconActivated` (+ companion `_beaconActivatedAt`) | `false` / `nil` at run start (bootstrap with the other PC tables, C.5.3) | on PC's `OnEscapeBeaconActivated(activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` subscription — Crafting's BCT3 window-START cue (C.5.8); set ONCE, never re-set within a run; the same statement records `_beaconActivatedAt = getServerTime()` (C.11 seam) | **(primary)** on the inbound `RunEnded` broadcast (any outcome — victory/defeat; the C.9 inbound-subscription row is the receiving declaration), mirroring Crafting's `RunSession.beaconActivated` lifecycle (C.5.8, H.61); **(self-heal, R17-4)** OR when the per-Heartbeat guard finds `_beaconActivated and (getServerTime() − _beaconActivatedAt) > BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT` — see below |

While `_beaconActivated == true`, T7's whole-squad-wipe raise is SUPPRESSED for the entire post-activation lifecycle (the in-window wipe terminal is Crafting's BCT-DEFEAT, raised to the arbiter — C.5.8). The latch is a one-way set within a run and is released **primarily** by the terminal `RunEnded` (the inbound subscription, C.9); H.61 asserts that release so it cannot ratchet across run boundaries (Pillar-4 risk if it stuck `true`).

**Bounded-timeout self-heal — `BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT` (R17-4, user-ruled 2026-06-14; closes round-19 game-B5 / #3b).** The `RunEnded` broadcast is the intended clear-path, but the arbiter is unauthored (F.4) and a dropped or never-sent broadcast would leave `_beaconActivated` stuck `true` — T7 permanently suppressed, the run unable to end through PC's wipe terminal (a Pillar-4 "the run can always end" violation). PC therefore **self-heals**: a single server-internal guard (folded into the existing reconciliation-tick Heartbeat connection, C.5.3 — no new connection) checks each scan whether `_beaconActivated and _beaconActivatedAt ~= nil and (getServerTime() − _beaconActivatedAt) > BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT`; if so it **forces `_beaconActivated = false` (and `_beaconActivatedAt = nil`), logs a `beacon-latch-timeout` warning with the squad/run context, and re-enables the T7 wipe terminal**. `BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT` is set comfortably above the longest legitimate Beacon window so it never fires during normal play: with the window bounded by ED's `BEACON_HALF_LIFE ∈ [64,128] s` (Crafting H.105 band `[35.0,70.1] s` of survival time) plus the `RUN_END_DEFEAT_HOLD` and arbiter latency, the longest legitimate `activation → RunEnded` span is well under 120 s; the knob defaults to **300 s** (G.6) — a ~2.5× margin that only ever trips on a genuinely lost broadcast. The self-heal is a **safety backstop, not a normal terminal**: when it fires the run-end is reached through the re-enabled T7 path, and any later straggler `RunEnded` is the idempotent no-op already specified in the C.9 inbound handler. H.61c asserts the timeout release; H.61 still asserts the primary `RunEnded` release.

### Interactions with Other Systems

#### C.7 — Touch-Input Detail

**Tap-hold gather** (PC + mobile contextual):

- Hold duration to commit = `TAP_HOLD_COMMIT_DURATION = 500 ms` *wall-clock* (not frames). 500 ms is the Apple HIG long-press floor; sits inside both 30 fps thermal-throttled mobile and 60 fps PC tolerances.
- **Wall-clock, not frame count, is mandatory** — defining in frames would make PC gather fire twice as fast as mobile.
- Progress ring fills client-side from T = 0 of the hold; completes at T = 500 ms. Releasing early cancels (no commit fired).
- On commit, client fires `RequestGather` to the server (server-confirmed). If the server rejects (out-of-range, depleted, dead), the ring animates to a "fail" state (brief red flash + dismiss).
- **The ring is the only client-predicted UI state in the controller.** All other feedback is server-confirmed.

**Proximity gate** (mobile contextual):

- Server-side Euclidean distance from `HumanoidRootPart.Position` to nearest gather-node center; threshold `GATHER_PROXIMITY_RADIUS = 4 studs`.
- Server fires `GatherNodeArmed` to the owning client when within range. Client renders a contextual prompt.
- Commit: single tap (touch) or `E` (PC) once the gate is armed. Fires `RequestGather` (server-confirmed).
- If the player moves out of range during the arm window, server fires `GatherNodeDisarmed`; client dismisses the prompt. No partial state is created.

**Two-finger quick-ping**:

- Detection window = `PING_TWO_FINGER_WINDOW = 150 ms`. Client tracks `UserInputService.TouchStarted` events; if two `TouchStarted` events land within this window (count, not position), the ping fires.
- **A third touch contact within the window cancels and suppresses the ping** — defends against frantic multi-finger sequences during predator encounters.
- Gesture detection lives in the client `PlayerControllerController` because `ContextActionService` does not natively express multi-touch gestures. Per-frame accumulator pattern.

**Mobile-touch analog-drift threshold**:

- The mobile thumbstick can output sub-perceptible drift values when held but unmoved. Per `ED.F.2a row 8` (Tier-2 #17), the stationary classifier MUST treat XZ-distance below `MOBILE_ANALOG_DRIFT_THRESHOLD = 2 studs` (over the previous pulse interval) as **not moving** for emission-attenuation purposes.
- This threshold is **distinct from `EMITTER_MOVEMENT_DELTA_FLOOR = 1 stud`** — the latter is the ED-side stationary gate; the former is a Player-Controller-side input-cleanup floor that filters drift before the position is used for the ED gate. **Rule**: on mobile, if the player's XZ-displacement over the previous pulse interval is below `MOBILE_ANALOG_DRIFT_THRESHOLD`, treat the player's effective displacement for emission-purposes as zero.
- **Silent classification** — no UI indicator for sub-threshold drift. Showing "you're not actually moving" would imply the player is trying to move and failing; visual character animation is sufficient feedback.

#### C.8 — Disturbance System Contract (publisher)

Player Controller is the **publisher of two emission types** to Ecological Disturbance per `ED.C.3.2` and `ED.F.2a` (canonical contract). The contract here is normative — any drift between this section and the ED GDD is a defect against ED, not against this GDD.

1. **Sprint emission** — fired by the server when:
   - The player enters S2 (Alive-Sprinting) — the **first pulse** fires within `FIRST_PULSE_GRACE_WINDOW = 1.0 s` of state-entry. Whether this first pulse is **grace-exempt** (full magnitude regardless of movement-delta) depends on the per-axis re-entry cooldown:
     - If `t_lastGracePulse_sprint` is `nil` (first sprint of this life) OR `(t_0 - t_lastGracePulse_sprint) >= GRACE_REENTRY_COOLDOWN`: **grace-exempt**. First pulse fires at `MAGNITUDE_SPRINT_PULSE = 0.10`. Server records `t_lastGracePulse_sprint = t_0`.
     - Otherwise (cooldown active): first pulse is **not grace-exempt**; falls through to magnitude-attenuation. Full magnitude fires if moving; attenuated magnitude fires if stationary.
   - Every `SPRINT_PULSE_INTERVAL = 2 s` thereafter while S2 is active.
   - Call: `DisturbanceService:Emit(emissionType="Sprint", position=playerWorldPosition, initialMagnitude=MAGNITUDE_SPRINT_PULSE, sourcePlayerId=playerId)`.
   - **Magnitude attenuation**: if XZ-displacement over the previous pulse interval is below `EMITTER_MOVEMENT_DELTA_FLOOR = 1 stud` AND the pulse is **NOT currently grace-exempt** (per the rule above — i.e., outside `FIRST_PULSE_GRACE_WINDOW` OR within it but cooldown-blocked), multiply `initialMagnitude` by `STATIONARY_EMISSION_FACTOR = 0.30`, **then floor the Sprint result at `SPRINT_HOLD_FLOOR = 0.09`** (`max(SPRINT_HOLD_FLOOR, 0.10 × 0.30) = 0.09`, R10-6 floor-and-feel raised per [R13-F1] — holding sprint stationary is itself loud; see D.4 Step 2). A stationary Sprint pulse therefore fires at **0.09**, not 0.03 — strictly above the lantern's 0.08 (the [R13-F1] hierarchy invariant, G.7) and strictly below the moving pulse's 0.10. **The moving(0.10)-vs-holding(0.09) gradient is made player-perceivable by the pulse-coincident sprint exertion audio layer (V/A.3, R12-game-B2)** — each Sprint emission pulse, moving or holding, plays an own-player UI-channel cue with a reduced-intensity stationary variant, so the player can hear that a stationary sprint still publishes.

   **Cooldown rationale (Pillar 1 closure)**: without `GRACE_REENTRY_COOLDOWN`, a player can toggle sprint ON for 0.9 s (grace-exempt first pulse), toggle OFF, wait 1.5 s `STAMINA_REGEN_DELAY` + ~0.225 s for stamina to regen, and toggle ON again for a fresh grace-exempt pulse — cycling at ~31% of continuous-sprint cost per disturbance unit (round-1 G1 exploit). The 6.0 s cooldown closes this cadence: legitimate sprint-rest-sprint patterns naturally exceed 6 s of walk; only micro-cadence exploits hit the cooldown.
2. **Light emission** — fired by the server when:
   - The player enters S3b (LanternRaised) — first pulse within `FIRST_PULSE_GRACE_WINDOW = 1.0 s`. Grace-exemption follows the same per-axis cooldown rule as Sprint, applied to the Light axis:
     - If `t_lastGracePulse_light` is `nil` OR `(t_0 - t_lastGracePulse_light) >= GRACE_REENTRY_COOLDOWN`: **grace-exempt**. First pulse fires at `MAGNITUDE_LIGHT_PULSE = 0.08`. Server records `t_lastGracePulse_light = t_0`.
     - Otherwise: not grace-exempt; falls through to magnitude-attenuation.
   - Every `LIGHT_PULSE_INTERVAL = 4 s` thereafter while S3b is active.
   - Call: `DisturbanceService:Emit(emissionType="Light", position=playerWorldPosition, initialMagnitude=MAGNITUDE_LIGHT_PULSE, sourcePlayerId=playerId)`.
   - Same magnitude-attenuation rule as Sprint (the gate applies whenever the pulse is not currently grace-exempt).

   **Cooldown rationale (Pillar 1 closure, Light parallel)**: without the cooldown, a player can toggle lantern on (grace pulse 0.08 fires) → off → on at the 200 ms `RequestLanternToggle` rate-limit floor → on again, producing up to ~2.5 grace pulses/s versus 1 pulse / 4 s for continuous lantern — round-1 review identified this as a **10× exploit factor**, worse than Sprint because the lantern has no stamina-coupled self-limit. The same 6.0 s cooldown closes both axes.

   **Per-axis independence**: `t_lastGracePulse_sprint` and `t_lastGracePulse_light` are tracked independently. Sprinting and raising the lantern within the same window do not interfere — each axis gates its own cooldown. The maximum-loud opening (sprint + lantern simultaneously on a fresh life) is permitted by design.
3. **Position semantics**: `playerWorldPosition` is the SERVER-observed `HumanoidRootPart.Position` at pulse-fire time. Client-predicted positions MUST NOT be used for emission position — the server's view is the authoritative input to Ecological Disturbance.
4. **Server clock (R12-build-B1)**: pulse intervals and grace-window timers MUST read server time **through the C.11 `getServerTime()` clock seam** — never an inline `workspace:GetServerTimeNow()` call at the call site (C.11 forbids and grep-gates exactly that call). The seam wraps `workspace:GetServerTimeNow()` in production, which keeps `ED.D.1` satisfied at the primitive level (`os.clock()`, `os.time()`, `tick()` remain forbidden).

#### C.9 — RemoteEvent Surface (introduced by this controller)

| Event | Direction | Payload | Rate Limit | Server Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RequestSprintToggle` | C → S | `{sprint: boolean}` | 10/s/player + burst cap 15 per 2.0 s (round-16 network — sustained-flood ceiling on top of the per-second limit; excess dropped silently) (debounced — same-state ignored; this event-specific guard does NOT apply to `PlayerHeartbeat`, which is a separate event with no payload) | Player alive; not in S4/S5; if requesting `sprint=true`, stamina > 0 |
| `RequestLanternToggle` | C → S | `{raised: boolean}` | 5/s/player + **burst cap 8 per 2.0 s** (round-20 NET-B1 — sustained-flood ceiling on top of the per-second limit; excess dropped silently) (**debounced — same-state ignored**: a `{raised}` equal to the player's current lantern-axis state is dropped before any side-effect, the same guard `RequestSprintToggle` already carries; this is exploit-parity, not an authority change) | Player alive; not in S4/S5. **Drop-first ordering (I1 parity)**: the rate-limit / burst / same-state checks evaluate BEFORE any state mutation or grace-anchor read — a dropped toggle MUST NOT touch `t_lastGracePulse_light` (else a flood of same-state toggles could perturb the C.8.2 grace cooldown). Verified by H.74. |
| `RequestPing` | C → S | `{rayOriginPos, rayDirection, candidateTargetId?}` | per-player 1.0 s + burst 3/1.5 s (per C.4.1) | **Alive only — reject from S4/S5 (dead-player ping CUT at MVP per R17-3, pending the Camera GDD; round-19 ux-B2/game-I6, C.5.3 item 4)**; ray origin within `PING_ORIGIN_TOLERANCE` of server-tracked position; server-side raycast result is authoritative (client `candidateTargetId` advisory); rate-limit |
| `RequestEmote` | C → S | `{slot: integer 1..6}` | per-player 3.0 s + burst 5/15 s (per C.4.2) | Alive OR dead-spectating; **`slot` MUST be an integer ∈ {1,2,3,4,5,6}** — reject non-integer (`slot % 1 ~= 0`), out-of-range, or non-number values (I4); rate-limit |
| `RequestGather` | C → S | `{nodeId}` (client-supplied) | per-player 2/s + burst 4/2.0 s (round-16 network — tap-hold commits are ≥ 500 ms apart by construction per C.7, so the limit is exploit headroom, not gameplay pacing) | **Alive-guard: reject from S4/S5/dead (C.5.4 — gather is a no-body input)**; `nodeId` MUST resolve to an existing, live gather node (reject unknown/stale ids silently); **server-side proximity check**: server-tracked `HumanoidRootPart.Position` within `GATHER_PROXIMITY_RADIUS = 4 studs` of the node center (the C.7 gate re-verified at commit — the client's armed-prompt state is never trusted). **Receive-side DEEP validation (depletion, yield, node state machine) lives in the unauthored Resource Node GDD — PC registers the trust boundary; RN owns deep validation (round-16 B5)** |
| `RequestInteract` | C → S | `{targetId}` (client-supplied) | per-player 2/s + burst 4/2.0 s (round-16 network — same shape as `RequestGather`) | **Alive-guard: reject from S4/S5/dead**; `targetId` MUST resolve to an existing interactable (bench, beacon — reject unknown/stale ids silently); **server-side range check**: server-tracked `HumanoidRootPart.Position` within `GATHER_PROXIMITY_RADIUS` of the target (C.7 proximity convention). **Receive-side DEEP validation lives in the Crafting receive-side surface — Crafting's C.15 declares its own named events (`RequestCraft`, `RequestBeaconActivate`, …) with per-event limits + a global per-player cap; `RequestInteract` here is PC's generic interact affordance pending the architecture-phase RemoteEvent-registry reconciliation ADR (round-16 B5)** |
| `PlayerHeartbeat` | C → S | `{}` (no payload) | `HEARTBEAT_SEND_RATE = 1 Hz` per player (server drops excess silently) | Player must be in S2 (Alive-Sprinting); if received while in S1/S4/S5, drop silently. **No same-state guard** — this event is not a state toggle. **Drop-first-write ordering (normative, I1)**: the rate-limit / state-validity check is evaluated BEFORE `_lastHeartbeatTime[player]` is written — a dropped (excess or wrong-state) event MUST NOT update the timestamp; the write happens only on the accept path (else flood traffic would extend the sprint-timeout window; verified by H.33b). |
| `OnSprintStateChanged` | S → C (broadcast) | `{playerId, sprinting: boolean}` | On transition only | n/a (server-pushed) |
| `OnLanternStateChanged` | S → C (broadcast) | `{playerId, raised: boolean}` | On transition only | n/a (server-pushed) |
| `OnStaminaChanged` | S → owning C only | `{stamina: 0.0..1.0}` | ≤ 5 Hz | n/a (server-pushed; HUD-only) |
| `OnSprintPulse` | S → owning C only | `{variant: "full" \| "hold"}` — **server-evaluated from the D.4 magnitude branch** (`"full"` = moving/grace pulse at 0.10; `"hold"` = stationary hold-floor pulse at `SPRINT_HOLD_FLOOR = 0.09`); the client never infers the variant | One event per Sprint emission pulse (every `SPRINT_PULSE_INTERVAL = 2 s` while S2 is active) — ~60–80 B/s at 4 sprinting players, pre-assessed architecturally sound (round-15 network) | n/a (server-pushed). **Round-16 B1 — the exertion-layer transport**: this event drives the V/A.3 sprint pulse exertion cue AND its V/A.4 caption equivalent; it carries no gameplay state and is never an input to anything server-side (H.57) |
| `OnPingBroadcast` | S → C (squad) | `{playerId, position, category, ttl}` | On valid ping only | n/a (validated before broadcast) |
| `OnEmoteBroadcast` | S → C (squad) | `{playerId, slot, ttl, renderScope}` — `renderScope ∈ {"world", "squad-ui"}` is a **server-minted dead-state discriminator (round-28 T-1/T-2, NET-2 closure)**: the server sets it from the emitter's server-tracked state at broadcast time — `"squad-ui"` when the emitter is in S4 (Dead-Respawning), `"world"` when alive. It is **server-authoritative and never client-supplied** (a client cannot forge it; `RequestEmote` carries only `slot`). HUD owns the render: `"world"` → the normal world-space billboard over the player; `"squad-ui"` → squad roster / spectator overlay ONLY, **no world-space billboard over the corpse** (the dead-position tell R17-3 cut S4 ping for — V/A.2, H.80). | On valid emote only | n/a (server-pushed; `renderScope` minted server-side from S4 state — the suppression is **server-driven**, not a client routing rule the exploit client could ignore: the broadcast carries no corpse position, and the authoritative render policy is expressed in the payload for HUD to obey) |
| `OnPlayerDied` | S → C (squad + dead) | `{playerId, deathEventId, deathTimestamp, deathCause, playerName}` — `deathEventId` is the server-minted monotonic event key from the `DeathCostReconciliation` record (**R13-build-N1**: V/A.4a's multi-death caption queue and H.52 dedupe captions by `deathEventId`, so the payload must carry it; clients treat it as an opaque dedup key, never as gameplay state); `deathTimestamp` is the record's death-time snapshot (**round-16 B2 — produced exactly once per death event** from the C.11 `getServerTime()` seam: Path-A handler entry / Path-B `cachedNow`; a `reconciliation-recovery` broadcast carries the ORIGINAL stamp, never the commit time — C.5.3, H.58); `deathCause` is an enumerated string ∈ `{"died"` (normal `Humanoid.Died`, Path A)`, "disconnect-while-damaged"` (Path B imminent-death disconnect)`, "reconciliation-recovery"` (delayed broadcast from the death-cost reconciliation tick, R6-I4)`}`. `playerName` carries the snapshot so recipients can render without a live instance. | On death only | **Recipient set (network B3)**: broadcast to the **currently-connected squad roster** at broadcast time — NOT to the departed player (Path B / reconciliation-recovery: the subject may have left the server). For `deathCause="reconciliation-recovery"` the subject `playerId` may have no live `Player` instance; recipients identify the subject by the `playerName`/`playerId` snapshot in the payload, never by resolving a live instance. Late-joiners who were not present at the original death receive nothing retroactively (HUD reconciles roster state from its own snapshot). **Late-joiner × `reconciliation-recovery` (network N-F2)**: a player who joins within the ≤10 s reconciliation window — after the original death but before the delayed `reconciliation-recovery` broadcast — would receive a `reconciliation-recovery` for a death they have no prior context for. HUD GDD MUST treat this as a **roster-confirm that the subject is dead/gone, NOT a fresh kill notification** (no Death-SFX-equivalent alarm). Recorded as a HUD contract obligation (F.4 HUD row). |
| `OnPlayerRespawned` | S → C (squad) | `{playerId, anchorPosition}` | On respawn only | n/a |
| `OnWorldResponseCue` | S → C (**living squad only**) | `{newTier}` — the new (higher) Ecological-Disturbance escalation tier index the squad just crossed INTO; **non-locating** (it is the squad-wide disturbance tier the HUD disturbance bar already shows — NO position, NO direction, NO distance, NO predator data) — lets HUD/PC optionally scale the swell by tier | One event per **upward** ED band-crossing only (PC fires it on receipt of the ED-owned `OnDisturbanceBandCrossed(direction="up", …)` inbound signal — never per pulse, never on a downward crossing; the hysteresis/debounce that prevents boundary machine-gunning is ED-owned at the producer) — **the R17-1 in-run loop-closer transport** (round-22 R21-1, closes round-19 #2 / round-21 #1b "no C.9 render surface"). Drives the V/A.3 world-response swell + V/A.4 caption | n/a (server-pushed; rendered to living squad only — a dead player's run-end is imminent, the cue is for the players still making loud/quiet choices) |

**The trust-boundary RemoteEvent registry from game-concept enumerates RE #4 (Ping), #5 (Emote), #7 (Respawn handshake), #10 (Death confirmation).** This GDD's `RequestPing` corresponds to #4; `RequestEmote` to #5; `OnPlayerRespawned` overlaps #7; `OnPlayerDied` overlaps #10. The architecture phase will reconcile via an ADR — see `Section F`.

**Resource-Node-owned events (cross-ref, round-16 network)**: `GatherNodeArmed(nodeId)` / `GatherNodeDisarmed(nodeId)` are **Resource-Node-owned S→C events** (F.2 RN row) consumed by PC's proximity-prompt UI (C.7) — they are registered and bandwidth-accounted in the RN GDD's surface, not in this table or H.29.

**Server-internal cross-service signals introduced by PC (NOT RemoteEvents — round-16)**: the following are Knit service-to-service calls/signals on the server only; none may appear on any `Service.Client` surface or be client-invokable (the same documentation-trap class as the `RequestSquadOxygenSpend` transport rewrite, C.5.3 step 3):

| Signal | Direction | Payload / arity | Fired when | Consumer |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RequestSquadOxygenSpend` | PC → RM | `(amount=1, reason="death", deathEventId)` — `deathEventId` is the cross-service idempotency key (R13-net-I1) | C.5.3 dispatch (initial T5 or reconciliation tick) | ResourceManagement (dedups per `(userId, deathEventId)`, F.2) |
| `RunController:RunEndConditionRaised` | PC → RunController | `(conditionType ∈ {"wipe","victory"}, squadState)` (F.4 caller-side contract) | T7 wipe (pre-activation only) / T8 victory | RunController arbiter ([R16-1] — sole `RunEnded` broadcaster) |
| `PredatorService:OnPlayerS4Entered` | PC → PredatorService | `(player)` | T5 S4-entry (Path A only; C.5.3 side-effect (h)) | PredatorService ([R16-2] — S4 no-re-acquire gate input) |
| `PredatorService:OnPlayerT6Respawned` | PC → PredatorService | `(player)` | T6 (C.6 — the single predator-table-clearance trigger channel + state-flush) | PredatorService ([R16-2]) |
| `PredatorService:ReleasePredatorLock` | PC → PredatorService | `(player)` | T5 initial dispatch, pre-yield, BOTH paths (C.5.3 step-2 side-effect (g)) + reconciliation-tick success (the R6-B8 (a)/(b)/(g) subset re-invokes it) | PredatorService (idempotent — releasing an already-released/never-held lock is a no-op, F.4 B-AI-2; the S4 no-re-acquire gate holds the release; H.51 covers the fire-side). **(round-28 build+qa convergence — this server-internal call was wired in C.5.3/C.6/F.4 but had no canonical-table row; its absence violated the C.12 invariant #1 and would have failed the C.12 CI hook on day one.)** |
| `OnSquadMemberAliveChanged` | PC → Crafting (squad-state subscribers) | `(playerId, isAlive)` | T5 (`isAlive=false`, both paths) / T6 after respawn placement (`isAlive=true`) | CraftingService `RunSession.aliveMembers` re-add (canonical publisher — Crafting C.10/F.4; H.72) |

**Server-internal INBOUND subscriptions consumed by PC (NOT RemoteEvents — round-20 keystone for `RunEnded` + round-22 R21-1 addition for `OnDisturbanceBandCrossed` + round-28 T-2 reconciliation completing the table to all FIVE)**: every signal in the outbound table above is one PC *raises*; PC also *subscribes* to **five** server-only signals (the round-28 template pass added rows (3)–(5), which were wired in the body — C.5.3 latch lifecycle, C.10, the C.5.1 oxygen-death trigger, H.48/H.61 — but undeclared in this table, the reconciliation-class miss build NEW-1 / round-26 flagged). **(1) `RunEnded`** (round-20 keystone, closes build-B1 / SD-19-B3, the round-19 load-bearing blocker), raised by the run-lifecycle arbiter — the receiving counterpart and the **only** declared clear-path for the `_beaconActivated` latch (C.6) and the **only** channel by which an in-window defeat reaches PC; without it the latch can never clear and the in-window-defeat S4→S5 transition is paper-only. Crafting declares its symmetric `RunEnded` subscription at its C.10 — PC was the asymmetric side; that row closes the asymmetry. **(2) `OnDisturbanceBandCrossed`** (round-22 R21-1), raised by Ecological Disturbance — the trigger PC re-broadcasts as the R17-1 world-response cue (`OnWorldResponseCue`, outbound table); PC owns only the up-filter + re-broadcast, never any perception logic. **(3) `OnEscapeBeaconActivated`** (Crafting BCT3 window-START cue — the SET trigger for the `_beaconActivated` run-lifecycle latch, C.6 latch table). **(4) `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`** (Crafting BCT4 window-end — the T8 victory trigger, C.10 Crafting row, H.48). **(5) `OnPlayerOxygenExpired`** (Resource Management — the C.5.1 death-flow trigger, C.10 RM row). All five are server-internal Knit signals; none appears on any `Service.Client` surface. **Deliberate NON-subscription (round-28 — recorded so the C.12 completeness hook and any reader's grep do not re-flag it as a missing row): PC does NOT subscribe to `OnBeaconWindowFailed`.** The in-window defeat is raised by Crafting's BCT-DEFEAT *directly* to the RunController arbiter; PC receives only the arbiter's `RunEnded(defeat)` (C.10 Crafting row, the NET-B2 no-client-influenced-run-end closure). `OnBeaconWindowFailed` is a Crafting HUD-only S→C cue — PC never consumes it, so its client-facing `reason` field never reaches a PC run-end authority decision. (Build NEW-1's "6th subscription" reading was a false positive: the body is self-consistent that PC consumes five, not six.)

| Signal | Direction | Payload / arity | Fired when | PC handler |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `RunEnded` | RunController → PC (**PC subscribes**) | `(outcome ∈ {"victory","defeat"})` | The arbiter resolves the single run-end broadcast ([R16-1] — sole broadcaster), once, after a `RunEndConditionRaised` from any source (PC's T7 wipe pre-activation, PC's T8 victory consuming `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`, or Crafting's BCT-DEFEAT in-window defeat raised directly to the arbiter) | **(1) CLEAR `_beaconActivated` → `false`** (C.6 latch table — this is the ONLY message-driven clear-path; H.61). **(2) Drive every player by `outcome`**: `defeat` → every alive-or-S4 player → **S5(defeat) + lock all inputs** (this IS the in-window-defeat S4→S5 path the C.6 T7 row points at); `victory` → every alive-or-S4 player → **S5(victory) + lock** (C.6 T8 — an S4 player is rescued and counted a survivor). **(3) Idempotent** — a second `RunEnded` within an already-resolved run is a no-op (latch already clear, all players already in S5). The handler accesses no client-supplied state — `outcome` originates server-side from the arbiter, never from a client `reason` field (the NET-B2 no-client-influenced-run-end property holds, C.10 Crafting row). **Bounded-timeout self-heal (R17-4, user-ruled 2026-06-14)** for the case where this broadcast never arrives: see the C.6 latch table CLEARED column + G.6 `BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT`. |
| `OnDisturbanceBandCrossed` | Ecological Disturbance → PC (**PC subscribes**) | `(direction ∈ {"up","down"}, newTier, timestamp)` — ED-owned, **non-positional/non-directional** (no source position, no predator data); hysteresis/debounce is ED's at the producer (F.4 — couples to ED Session B/C, same hysteresis class ED already owns for its tier transitions) | ED's squad-wide disturbance level crosses an escalation-tier threshold (the round-22 R21-1 world-response loop-closer trigger; ED owns the level + thresholds, ED.D/ED.G — this is NOT a per-pulse event and NOT a new PC perception path) | **On `direction == "up"` only**: fire `OnWorldResponseCue {newTier}` to the **living** squad (the C.9 outbound render event) → drives the V/A.3 swell + V/A.4 caption. A `"down"` (de-escalation) crossing fires nothing (the cue means "your noise raised the heat," never "it cooled"). PC adds no logic beyond the up-filter + re-broadcast; it never reads position or any predator-perception state. **Forward obligation**: the `OnDisturbanceBandCrossed` producer is ED-owned and unauthored — PC's subscription + render is buildable against the named arity now; the producer cadence/hysteresis lands in ED Session B/C (F.4). |
| `OnEscapeBeaconActivated` | Crafting → PC (**PC subscribes**) | `(activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` — Crafting-owned (the BCT3 window-START cue) | Crafting's beacon activates (BCT3), once per run | **SET the `_beaconActivated` run-lifecycle latch → `true`** (the C.6 latch table SET column — set ONCE, never re-set within a run) and record `_beaconActivatedAt = getServerTime()` (C.11 seam). This is the latch whose `true` value suppresses the T7 whole-squad-wipe raise for the post-activation lifecycle (C.5.8/C.6 T7). PC adds no logic beyond the latch set + timestamp snapshot; the activation itself is Crafting-authored. **PC IGNORES the payload's `windowDurationSeconds` (round-28 network-IMPORTANT): the `BEACON_LATCH_TIMEOUT` self-heal uses the server-config constant (G.6), NOT this payload field — PC computes no expected window-end from it and derives no suppression duration from it, so a payload-supplied duration can neither shorten nor extend T7 suppression. The field is carried only for any Crafting/HUD-side use, never read by PC's latch logic.** Verified by H.61 (SET trigger) / H.61b (bootstrap false-init). |
| `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` | Crafting → PC (**PC subscribes**) | `(timestamp)` — Crafting-owned (the BCT4 window-END survival result; NOT `OnEscapeBeaconActivated`/BCT3, which is window-start only) | Crafting's beacon survival window resolves a win (BCT4 window-end), once per run | **Drive T8 victory** — every alive-or-S4 player → S5(victory) + lock (C.6 T8; an S4 player is rescued and counted a survivor, H.48). PC raises `RunController:RunEndConditionRaised("victory", squadState)` to the arbiter (the outbound table) and the arbiter's `RunEnded(victory)` then drives the S5 transition; PC consumes the window-survived signal, never broadcasts the run-end itself ([R16-1]). |
| `OnPlayerOxygenExpired` | Resource Management → PC (**PC subscribes**) | `(playerId)` — RM-owned (squad oxygen pool reaches 0 *and* the per-player oxygen-grace timer expires, C.5.1) | RM's oxygen-grace timer expires for a player whose squad pool is empty | **Trigger the C.5 death flow (T5)** for that player (the Path-A-equivalent oxygen-death entry). RM owns the trigger condition + grace timer (RM GDD, F.2/C.10 RM row); PC owns the death-flow handler it gates. Forward obligation: the RM trigger is unauthored — PC's subscription is buildable against the named arity now (C.10 RM row, F.2). |
